We have pointed out to her a number of subjects, important for us, during the consultations, but we have not received a single answer on the subject of immigration: nothing on the subject of security; nothing on the subject of justice; no reference to the web tax; nothing on what will be of the income of citizenship, which costs EUR 8 billion; on what we will do for recruitment, since a decree of dignity paralyzes and casts the world of work; nothing on the dramatic theme of the ristors to enterprises. President Draghi, ours is not a choice for spite or for a spirit of contradiction or - as someone foolishly said - to choose the easiest way. The opposite is true: we choose the most difficult path, so it is true that we walk it alone or almost. [[Applause]] The path we have chosen, Mr Draghi, is the path of coherence: coherence towards ourselves, first of all, and coherence towards voters. You probably do not know that, Mr Draghi, but about three years ago we, like now, signed a pact with our voters, with the citizens. It said in that pact that we would commit ourselves to creating a center-right alternative. So, never to the government with the Democratic Party, never with the Movimento 5 Stars. [[Applause]] I would also like to remind you of some passages in your speech, which I have here with me, in which you quote the Republican spirit and the best values of Italy. We believe that the Republican spirit and the best values of this country are first and foremost being built by saying and repeating in this House that in life, as in politics, promises are maintained and the word given must always be honoured, no matter what it costs. [[Applause]] We do not expect our opponents to applaud us for our decision. We do not ask for anyone's consent: everyone makes his own choices and they are all legitimate choices. We believe, however, that we deserved respect. In a political landscape in which parties and leaders say one thing on Monday, Tuesday another thing, Wednesday something still different and so on for every day, there is a party and a leader who simply keep the point: they do not sell and do not get bought. [[Applause]] They do not haggle with anyone: they do not blackmail anyone, they simply act and work in the sunlight, as we are used to doing. We are the right of government. We are proudly governing power in many regions and in many municipalities. We are in Europe where we are included in the Group of European Conservatives and our leader is also its president. But we are not right-wing government with any government and at any cost. We are truly convinced that governing with groups and with people who have not only different ideas, but opposed to ours is not only a betrayal of coherence, but also wrong, useless and ultimately harmful to the country. [[Applause]] The demand was and remains that of elections immediately. Do not tell me that asking for elections is a gesture of irresponsibility, because in democracy giving back the word to the citizens is never wrong. Now we would have a strong and cohesive government, hopefully for us as center-right, but with the difference that that government would be voted and chosen by the citizens. [[Applause]] Is there anyone, especially among the centre-left benches, who always gives lessons in democracy, who thinks that the call for responsibility means a Parliament without opposition, without free voices, criticism and dissent? I listened with these ears in the summer of 2019 and last December the parliamentarians of the left shout to be careful of the full powers whether it was Salvini or Conte. Now, on the other hand, there is no one who cares about a Parliament made up of only a thousand Members. [[Applause]] We take the heavy task of representing the opposition in this country, which is as important as those who govern. Then there are the intelligent people who explain to us every day in the newspapers and on television that this is politics and that politics is the art of the possible. Of course, it is the art of the possible, but not of the impossible because not even politics can transform opposites into equals. And we, President Draghi, have had the demonstration in this legislature first with the unnatural attempt of a yellow-green alliance, ended badly, and then with the yellow-red alliance, ended even worse, that is, with the shame of the Senate turned into a market. [[Applause]] Now you want to explain to us that the sum of these failures is a credible alternative for this country? Then you also explain to us that this is a technical, institutional or other government. The truth that we all know is that there are only political governments and that too is so, like all the others. It is so political that we have seen the discussions between the parties to indicate the Ministers; we have seen a new Government composed of so many old faces and in continuity with Count II that it resembles a Count III. In addition, in this Government the PD and the left as a whole dictate the rules, hold the keys of this majority and control directly or indirectly six very important ministries of spending. It is so true that this was a political choice. And, if you have a minimum of intellectual honesty, you should confess that the general enthusiasm that you experienced a few weeks ago compared to a Dragons that formed the Government of the best with the dream team quickly turned into a burning disappointment. But it is also said that it is now a matter of being serious, of being responsible and that we cannot escape, because there is to save Italy. But, excuse me: do you want to save Italy with those who have buried it? With those who took her to the abyss? [[Applause]] With those who have spent 150 billion euros and still have not managed to restore entire production chains? President Draghi, let me make a point that seems provocative, but it is not. I expected you to find at least 30 seconds in an hour of intervention to remember the despair of mountain entrepreneurs, who have been on their knees and have not seen a lira for a year. [[Applause]] You told them to open, with very strict rules, and then, four hours after the reopening of the lifts, you said you were joking. I wanted to hear clear words from you and your Government, because this is no longer a question of rejoicing: this is about compensating for the damage you have caused to these economic categories that remain - I repeat - in desperation and seems also in the disinterest of the Government. I have almost finished my time, Mr President, and I must start at the end of my speech. I just want to say that, among the many things that I do not share, there is the management of health and I want to make an appeal to you bipartisan: it is possible that out of 60 million Italians there is no better than the ineffable Commissioner Arcuri [[Applause]] , the inventor of primulous pavilions, the greatest expert in waste of public money, the one who deals with everything wrong? In conclusion, we will be on different sides and we will try to help our country on different sides. We're not gonna be a servile opposition, and we're gonna have our backs straight. We will be a patriotic opposition, without prejudice and - believe me - completely disinterested. For many reasons, many of which I have not had time to explain, we will be convinced of the opposition of your Government, but we will never be against the interests of Italy. [[Applause.]]

It is the opportunity of innovation, to create quality jobs. It is the covenant with our children, with future generations. You have to accompany, solicit, make sure you can go out with what? With the possibility of a more just society. This word is sacred to me, because the current pandemic has highlighted the inequalities that we already had: gender, territorial, social. Therefore, the challenge we face today is to take up inequalities on our chests. There is no possibility of the future if we do not do so, if this does not become obsession, the guide of our action. Will it be simple? I don't think so. And the dignity of work goes hand in hand with conversion because that is the model on which we must aim: a new model of development, a new humanity, a new relationship between human beings and the rest of creation. This is what we must do obsessively; it is not a question of technocrats or technology, it is a question of political vision that must be able to guide. I have chosen this example because I believe it is emblematic of an orientation towards a corporate vision of the country, of the school and of the health; a vision from which instead today we need to get away, because the companies give priority to the profit at all costs. This government will have to give priority to all Italians, to that network of micro-enterprises that constitute the true fabric of the Italian territory and that today are in difficulty and will be in the future, if we do not accompany them towards the transition. Talking about human capital is like talking about natural capital, like all the resources that nature offers us to produce services and goods for man, so still profit. Of course there must be profit, but without irreversibly threatening nature, our home. We need, therefore, a programme that sees all the crucial sectors that make up our society related, where the ecological economy puts itself at the service of the protection of the balances of nature, while at the same time embracing the social balances and the rights of people. You, Mr President-in-Office of the Council, spoke of ecological transition, which in my opinion should not be bound by the obvious limits of green capitalism. In this regard we await the regulation of taxonomy for the classification of sustainable production activities. Nuclear? The gas? The capture of industrial CO2? The energy from the garbage? But if the ecological transition is understood by you only as a matter of energy markets and sectors and not as an integrated and cross-cutting strategy that aims at a sustainable development model, you will not go very far and we will end up living the so-called greenwashing. In conclusion, I do not think that a Government of all can be a Government on behalf of the Italians; instead I see a Government in which the consent of all delegates the decision to a few. I cannot judge them, but I can analyze the numbers, and I know that I am facing the prospect of such a heterogeneous majority that it becomes difficult even to agree that, at this moment, what we breathe is air; a vast majority with also political forces inside that for us - which once, at least, in this we were "us" - represent the antivalues that we have always fought, where my parliamentary strength, my Movimento 5 Stars, is actually a minority. On the subject, there are some who are divisive even within the newly formed interparliamentary group, so much so that this cannot offer real guarantees and deny it is blind. Just as we have not had certain guarantees on the rules and paths already started, first of all the income of citizenship and the reform of the prescription, while it is certain that the super Ministry of the ecological transition is not at all super and that the Ministry of Economic Development is at our antipodes. For all these reasons, Mr President, and for a thousand others that I could list, if I had time, and despite... [[The microphone is automatically switched off. Applause.]]

ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u224	I ask you to speak for an explanation of vote in dissent from my Group.
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u225	I take note of that and give you the floor.
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u226	DRAGO (Mixed). Mr President, before any membership comes the duty of citizenship: these are words spoken by you this morning, Mr Draghi. I crossed that threshold convinced I would give my support and trust to the government. I have listened to all the speeches, clearly within the limits of my possibilities, and I must say that I do not feel like closing the door completely, but I do not feel like opening it in the same way. There is talk of ecological transition and attention to ecology. The etymology of the word is from oikos and logos. Oikos has two meanings: environment and home. Who should the house be inhabited by? I do not understand why there is continuity with previous governments in not considering the key issue, that of demographic decline. If you go to see all the studies, you see the correspondence of the curves of an economic crisis with the demographic decrease: it is a fact. As far as recovery plan is concerned, it is necessary to submit a project on this subject. Another question: there is much talk of teachers, of the 120,000 recruits, therefore of the precarious, but one never mentions a question that has ancient roots, before the so-called law on good school (No. 107 of 2015); we have teachers who for five, ten, fifteen or twenty years ask to return to their territories. Speaking of bond is a non-meaning; the term itself is also an obnoxious one. It must be borne in mind that teachers must be able to work in an area close to their residence, also for logical issues of transmission of traditions, knowledge and territorial stories. Mr President-in-Office of the Council, I have a very high regard for you and also for some ministers (not all of them clearly: as in class councils, we cannot have all the best professors). The initial opening was there, but at this point I manifested my abstention from the vote. [[Comments.]]
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u227	I'm asking to talk.
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u228	Senator Ciampollo, for what? CHAMPOLILLO (Mixed). I ask you to speak for an explanation of vote in dissent from my Group. PRESIDENT. - The debate is closed. She wasn't actually registered, but she always comes to the last moment... CHAMPOLLO (Mist). Mr President, I have come and signed up. I didn't sign up at the last minute and I didn't get here now. PRESIDENT. - The debate is closed. All right, Senator Ciampollo, I'll take note of that and give you the floor. CHAMPOLILLO (Mixed). Mr President, Senator Salvini, who I believe has never worked in his life, continues to offend my person for having voted for the trust of the Count Government, supported by the Movimento 5 Stars, in which I have been militant since his constitution and even before, having personally founded the first meeting of Friends of Beppe Grillo in Puglia. Salvini himself, with his bare torso, showed his vulgarity to Papeete; he hated Rome and the South; he claimed to work on Radio Padania, an unauthorized radio on the 740 channel of terrestrial digital. I mean, that Salvini justifies his vote by announcing my end. Being bearers of ideas and initiatives that are not in line with dominant thinking cannot justify the vicious and violent campaign of hatred and falsehood that has been cleverly carried out against me by this sort of crooked politician, a clown.
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u229	[[Comments.]] Just listen to his words, like those of Porro and Briatore, to understand the ethical and moral level of these people and the political area they represent.
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u230	Watch the terms.
ParlaMint-IT_2021-02-17-LEG18-Senato-sed-298.ana.u231	CHAMPOLILLO (Mixed). Rather, I have to clarify some positions on which I have been attacked ignorantly and without any possibility of replying. [[Comments.]] First of all, xylella: for years in Salento a special detergent is used to protect the olive trees from the bacterium; a simple remedy and perhaps for this object of ridicule, even if it has been validated by recent scientific studies of the University of Bari. [[Comments.]] I never said that xylella doesn't exist or that it cures with a shampoo. It is the cut of the olive trees that serves nothing, considering that the bacterium is present on over thirty-five species of plants. What, then, would they like to cement everything? Then the use of medical cannabis as a remedy for the deadly effects of Covid-19: medical cannabis is a powerful anti-inflammatory and pain reliever without contraindications, used in Italy in the treatment of serious diseases. Already a year ago I invited Minister Hope and some researchers to deepen the topic, without success. Well, today the scientific publication of the Medical College of Georgia in the United States attests how CBD, the principle of cannabis, reduces the cytokinic storm triggered by the immune system, attenuating and avoiding the most serious damage to lung tissue and artificial ventilation. Moreover, it is not only the virus that afflicts the citizens; lockdown, social distancing, masks and curfew are devastating the psyche and annihilating the social rights won by effort and with the blood of our predecessors. Anticovid policies are being implemented in the world much less restrictive and with better results. In Sweden not a single day of lockdown and there is no obligation to mask; in Belarus all activities are open, including discos; Germany's hard lockdown, even very hard, does not include curfew and masks are not mandatory outdoors; in Holland the International Court of Justice has just declared the illegality of night curfew. The results show deaths in line with normality and without house arrests of citizens. Having said that, it is necessary to restore and respect the inviolable rights of citizens by putting the Constitution first. Because of the disagreement about the irreversibility of the euro, about the consistency with principles and also about the presence of people like Salvini, I announce my vote against, so I reserve the right to evaluate the individual measures for the good of citizens and not of parties. [[Applause]] CHORO OF VOICES. Ciampolillo, Ciampolillo!

As stated by the Conference of Leaders, which I am not seeing, each Senator will vote exclusively from his place, declaring his vote. I remember, for the umpteenth time, that it is necessary to avoid gatherings at the center of the hemicycle. The senators in favour of the trusted motion will answer yes; the opposing senators will answer no; the senators who intend to abstain will respond accordingly. I now draw by lot the name of the senator from whom the roll call will begin. (The name of Senator Garruti is drawn by lot). I invite the Senator Secretary to proceed with the appeal, starting with Senator Garruti. Mr Durnwalder, Secretary, is appealing.
